=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @cache
        def fn(i, x):
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."
            if x < 0 or i = len(toppingCosts): return 0
            return min(fn(i+1, x), toppingCosts[i] + fn(i+2, x-toppingCosts[i]), key=lambda y: (abs(y-x), y))
        
        ans = infinity
        for bc in baseCosts: 
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda x: (abs(x-target), x))
        return ans

class Solution:

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            skip = fn(i + 1, x)
            take = toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i])
            return min(skip, take, key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y))

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda y: (abs(y - target), y))
        return ans
```

Let's maybe write final code:

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0

            skip = fn(i + 1, x)
            take = toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i])
            return min(skip, take, key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y))

        ans = float("inf")
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda y: (abs(y - target), y))
        return ans
```

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0

            skip = fn(i + 1, x)
            take = toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i])
            return min(skip, take, key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y))

        ans = float("inf")
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda y: (abs(y - target), y))
        return ans
```

from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:

            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):

        ans = float("inf")

=== RAW ANSWER ===
with the requested format.

```python
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i >= len(toppingCosts):
                return 0

            skip = fn(i + 1, x)
            take = toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i])
            return min(skip, take, key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y))

        ans = float("inf")
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda y: (abs(y - target), y))
        return ans
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i >= len(toppingCosts):
                return 0

            skip = fn(i + 1, x)
            take = toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i])
            return min(skip, take, key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y))

        ans = float("inf")
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda y: (abs(y - target), y))
        return ans
